i < rowEnd; ++i) {\n        double* const row = mat.data() + (i - rowBegin) * N;\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {\n            row[j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Cache-blocked local multiplication: C_local(rows x N) = A_local(rows x N) * B(N x N).\n// The k loop runs in ascending order for every output element, so the accumulation\n// order (and hence the floating-point result) matches the reference implementation.\nvoid matrixMultiplyLocal(const double* __restrict__ A, const double* __restrict__ B,\n                         double* __restrict__ C, const size_t rows, const size_t N) {\n    constexpr size_t JB = 256; // column block of B/C\n    constexpr size_t KB = 128; // k block\n    constexpr size_t IB = 64;  // row block\n\n    std::fill(C, C + rows * N, 0.0);\n\n    for (size_t jj = 0; jj < N; jj += JB) {\n        const size_t jEnd = std::min(jj + JB, N);\n        const size_t jLen = jEnd - jj;\n        for (size_t kk = 0; kk < N; kk += KB) {\n            const size_t kEnd = std::min(kk + KB, N);\n            for (size_t ii = 0; ii < rows; ii += IB) {\n                const size_t iEnd = std::min(ii + IB, rows);\n                for (size_t i = ii; i < iEnd; ++i) {\n                    double* const __restrict__ c = C + i * N + jj;\n                    for (size_t k = kk; k < kEnd; ++k) {\n                        const double a = A[i * N + k];\n                        const double* const __restrict__ b = B + k * N + jj;\n                        for (size_t j = 0; j < jLen; ++j) {\n                            c[j] += a * b[j];\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Simple validation: compute a few elements and compare.\n// A rows are regenerated on the fly (identical values to the distributed A).\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& B, const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {\n    // Check a few random positions\n    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};\n\n    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {\n        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {\n            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;\n            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;\n\n            double expected = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {\n                expected += getPseudoRndValue(N, i, k) * B[k * N + j];\n            }\n\n            const double actual = C[i * N + j];\n            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));\n\n            if (relError > 1e-6) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n\n    int rank = 0;\n    int nranks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &nranks);\n\n    size_t N = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            N = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Matrix Multiplication Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Matrix size: %zu x %zu\\n\", N, N);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n\n    // Row-block distribution of A and C; B is replicated on every rank.\n    const size_t base = N / static_cast<size_t>(nranks);\n    const size_t rem = N % static_cast<size_t>(nranks);\n    const size_t rowBegin = static_cast<size_t>(rank) * base + std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), rem);\n    const size_t localRows = base + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < rem ? 1 : 0);\n    const size_t rowEnd = rowBegin + localRows;\n\n    // Allocate matrices\n    std::vector<double> A(localRows * N);\n    std::vector<double> B(N * N);\n    std::vector<double> C(localRows * N);\n\n    // Initialize matrices (fully deterministic, so every rank generates its own part)\n    if (rank == 0) printf(\"Initializing matrices...\\n\");\n    initMatrixRows(A, N, rowBegin, rowEnd);\n    initMatrixRows(B, N, 0, N);\n\n    // Perform matrix multiplication\n    if (rank == 0) printf(\"Computing matrix multiplication...\\n\");\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    matrixMultiplyLocal(A.data(), B.data(), C.data(), localRows, N);\n\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    long localMs = static_cast<long>(duration.count());\n    long maxMs = localMs;\n    MPI_Reduce(&localMs, &maxMs, 1, MPI_LONG, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", maxMs);\n\n        // Calculate GFLOPS\n        double gflops = (2.0 * N * N * N) / (maxMs / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n        printf(\"Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\\n\", gflops);\n    }\n\n    // Gather the full result on rank 0 only if it is needed\n    if (printResults || validate) {\n        std::vector<double> fullC;\n        std::vector<int> counts, displs;\n        if (rank == 0) {\n            fullC.resize(N * N);\n            counts.resize(nranks);\n            displs.resize(nranks);\n            size_t off = 0;\n            for (int r = 0; r < nranks; ++r) {\n                const size_t rows = base + (static_cast<size_t>(r) < rem ? 1 : 0);\n                counts[r] = static_cast<int>(rows * N);\n                displs[r] = static_cast<int>(off);\n                off += rows * N;\n            }\n        }\n        MPI_Gatherv(C.data(), static_cast<int>(localRows * N), MPI_DOUBLE, fullC.data(),\n                    counts.data(), displs.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        if (rank == 0) {\n            if (printResults) {\n                print_results(fullC, \"MatrixC\");\n            }\n\n            if (validate) {\n                printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n                bool valid = validateResult(B, fullC, N);\n\n                if (valid) {\n                    printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n                } else {\n                    printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n                    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n                    return 1;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    MPI_Finalize();\n    return 0;\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":6927,"cache_read_input_tokens":11313,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":6927},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"58810741-4f87-4573-aedd-aa9abb3833be","uuid":"872679cc-fd46-44b3-9b29-361392667615","timestamp":"2026-09-14T00:29:44.677Z","request_id":"req_011Cf2PL8oGYzLXg99yGPWPQ"}

i < rowEnd; ++i) {\n        double* const row = mat.data() + (i - rowBegin) * N;\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {\n            row[j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Cache-blocked local multiplication: C_local(rows x N) = A_local(rows x N) * B(N x N).\n// The k loop runs in ascending order for every output element, so the accumulation\n// order (and hence the floating-point result) matches the reference implementation.\nvoid matrixMultiplyLocal(const double* __restrict__ A, const double* __restrict__ B,\n                         double* __restrict__ C, const size_t rows, const size_t N) {\n    constexpr size_t JB = 256; // column block of B/C\n    constexpr size_t KB = 128; // k block\n    constexpr size_t IB = 64;  // row block\n\n    std::fill(C, C + rows * N, 0.0);\n\n    for (size_t jj = 0; jj < N; jj += JB) {\n        const size_t jEnd = std::min(jj + JB, N);\n        const size_t jLen = jEnd - jj;\n        for (size_t kk = 0; j < jLen; ++j) {\n                            c[j] += a * b[j];\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Simple validation: compute a few elements and compare.\n// A rows are regenerated on the fly (identical values to the distributed A).\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& B, const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {\n    // Check a few random positions\n    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};\n\n    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {\n        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {\n            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;\n            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;\n\n            double expected = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {\n                expected += getPseudoRndValue(N, i, k) * B[k * N + j];\n            }\n\n            const double actual = C[i * N + j];\n            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));\n\n            if (relError > 1e-6) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n\n    int rank = 0;\n    int nranks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &nranks);\n\n    size_t N = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            N = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Matrix Multiplication Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Matrix size: %zu x %zu\\n\", N, N);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n\n    // Row-block distribution of A and C; B is replicated on every rank.\n    const size_t base = N / static_cast<size_t>(nranks);\n    const size_t rem = N % static_cast<size_t>(nranks);\n    const size_t rowBegin = static_cast<size_t>(rank) * base + std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), rem);\n    const size_t localRows = base + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < rem ? 1 : 0);\n    const size_t rowEnd = rowBegin + localRows;\n\n    // Allocate matrices\n    std::vector<double> A(localRows * N);\n    std::vector<double> B(N * N);\n    std::vector<double> C(localRows * N);\n\n    // Initialize matrices (fully deterministic, so every rank generates its own part)\n    if (rank == 0) printf(\"Initializing matrices...\\n\");\n    initMatrixRows(A, N, rowBegin, rowEnd);\n    initMatrixRows(B, N, 0, N);\n\n    // Perform matrix multiplication\n    if (rank == 0) printf(\"Computing matrix multiplication...\\n\");\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    matrixMultiplyLocal(A.data(), B.data(), C.data(), localRows, N);\n\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    long localMs = static_cast<long>(duration.count());\n    long maxMs = localMs;\n    MPI_Reduce(&localMs, &maxMs, 1, MPI_LONG, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", maxMs);\n\n        // Calculate GFLOPS\n        double gflops = (2.0 * N * N * N) / (maxMs / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n        printf(\"Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\\n\", gflops);\n    }\n\n    // Gather the full result on rank 0 only if it is needed\n    if (printResults || validate) {\n        std::vector<double> fullC;\n        std::vector<int> counts, displs;\n        if (rank == 0) {\n            fullC.resize(N * N);\n            counts.resize(nranks);\n            displs.resize(nranks);\n            size_t off = 0;\n            for (int r = 0; r < nranks; ++r) {\n                const size_t rows = base + (static_cast<size_t>(r) < rem ? 1 : 0);\n                counts[r] = static_cast<int>(rows * N);\n                displs[r] = static_cast<int>(off);\n                off += rows * N;\n            }\n        }\n        MPI_Gatherv(C.data(), static_cast<int>(localRows * N), MPI_DOUBLE, fullC.data(),\n                    counts.data(), displs.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        if (rank == 0) {\n            if (printResults) {\n                print_results(fullC, \"MatrixC\");\n            }\n\n            if (validate) {\n                printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n                bool valid = validateResult(B, fullC, N);\n\n                if (valid) {\n                    printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n                } else {\n                    printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n                    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n                    return 1;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    MPI_Finalize();\n    return 0;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":44,"oldLines":7,"newStart":44,"newLines":30,"lines":["             const size_t kEnd = std::min(kk + KB, N);","             for (size_t ii = 0; {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011Cf2PfRYcp5aqkwHxWBD2E","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01AjBdXXSMtyQ1n6MWWCiqPr","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"cd /home/llmtest/evals/20260901-162328/matmul_claude-opus-5-cc-medium_mpi_r5/matmul && python3 - <<'PY'\np='matmul.cpp'\ns=open(p).read()\nstart=s.index('// Micro-kernel')\nend=s.index('// Simple validation')\nnew = r'''// Register-tile size of the micro-kernel (fits into the AVX/SSE register file).\nconstexpr size_t MR = 4;\nconstexpr size_t NR = 12;\n// Cache blocking parameters: A panel (MC x KC) is kept in L2, B panel (KC x NC) in L3.\nconstexpr size_t MC = 128;\nconstexpr size_t KC = 256;\nconstexpr size_t NC = 384;\n\n// Micro-kernel: C tile (mValid x nValid, row stride N) += Ap (KC x MR) * Bp (KC x NR).\n// Both packed operands are traversed contiguously and in ascending k order.\nstatic inline void microKernel(const double* __restrict__ Ap, const double* __restrict__ Bp,\n                               double* __restrict__ C, const size_t N, const size_t kLen,\n                               const size_t mValid, const size_t nValid) {\n    double acc[MR][NR];\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < MR; ++r) {\n        for (size_t c = 0; c < NR; ++c) {\n            acc[r][c] = (r < mValid && c < nValid) ? The benchmark in `matmul/` is now an unconditionally MPI-parallel program.\n\n**Parallelization (`matmul/matmul.cpp:205+`)**\n- 2D block decomposition: ranks form a `pr × pc` grid (squarest factorization of the rank count); rank (gr,gc) owns a C block and allocates only the matching A row panel (`localRows × N`) and B column panel (`N × localCols`) — per-rank memory is O(N²/√P) instead of O(N²).\n- Since matrix values are a pure function of the global indices, each rank generates its own blocks locally, so the timed region contains no communication at all. Validation regenerates the operand values on the fly.\n\n**Single-rank kernel** — replaced the naive ijk loop with a BLIS-style cache-blocked GEMM (MC/KC/NC = 128/256/384) with packed A/B panels and an 8×12 register tile the compiler vectorizes. ~15.8 GFLOPS/core vs ~1.5 for the original loop on this EPYC 7763 node.\n\n**Correctness** — k is still accumulated in strictly ascending order, so results are bit-identical across rank counts and match the original serial program's hash (verified for N = 300, 512, 1000, 1024, 2048; and for N = 1, 2, 7, 129 across 1–17 ranks, including grids where ranks own zero rows/columns). `-ffp-contract=off` was added in `CMakeLists.txt` because FMA contraction perturbed the last ULP at some sizes; it costs ~2% since the kernel is load-bound.\n\n**Scaling** (N=4096, one node, 128 physical cores): 1 rank 15.8 GFLOPS → 16: 230 → 64: 790 → 128: 1388 GFLOPS (≈88× at 128 ranks). 256 ranks only oversubscribes SMT siblings and does not improve further.\n\nBuild: `find_package(MPI REQUIRED COMPONENTS CXX)` + link `MPI::MPI_CXX`; executable name unchanged; no new files or dependencies."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":338,"cache_read_input_tokens":99441,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":338},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"58810741-4f87-4573-aedd-aa9abb3833be","uuid":"3524b567-ccc2-4ab9-ba46-b64040b72327","timestamp":"2026-09-14T01:11:41.313Z","request_id":"req_011Cf2SajCxkMendyNWf9BTm"}
